| +2 weeks | Buffer |

**Week 1 status (2026-10-14).** Built and verified:
- **Tokens:** the real Figma export (9 read-only `use_figma` calls, TokensBrücke CLI 3.18.0) through the normalizer (both prefixes, variable-id identity, golden-tested) and a Terrazzo 2.8.0 dry run: 11 source files, 200 tokens, `tz check` clean. Emitted names, TokensBrücke limits and open items: `packages/tokens/figma/DRY-RUN.md`. The `codeSyntax` rewrite was planned in week 1 and applied to the Figma file on 2026-10-14, together with the Grove → Nizivo rename of collections and styles (M10; figma-handoff §1.4 #1).
- **Code:** minimal `nz gen` (barrels, `engine.ts` from `<Name>Engine`, exports, component map), the spring generator, Button, and Select, Dialog and Tooltip on React Aria 1.22.1 with `engineDom` and `stateAttrs()`. `packages/react` ships a `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES` file for its inlined glyphs, and a unit test checks it ([§14.2](#142-icons-nizivoicons)).
- **Floor lane** (`fixtures/floor-lane/RESULTS.md`): Firefox 129.0 passes the S1 nesting matrix, the halo/inverse probe and `@starting-style` overlay entry on the token path. It records Deviations page entries D1 and D2 ([§18.2](#182-docs-site-public)) and the inverse-inside-inverse gap ([§7.4](#74-theme-mechanism)). Safari 17.5 is not tested.
- **Next fixture** (`fixtures/next/RESULTS.md`): on the packed packages (the unpacked `pnpm pack` output, [§18.1](#181-test-layers)) and the generated barrels, Turbopack, webpack and webpack + `optimizePackageImports` all pass (Chromium). Plain webpack reproduces the S2c barrel leak and `optimizePackageImports` removes it, as [§11.1](#111-per-framework-setup-and-gotchas) says.
- **Exit criteria:** advances 2 (the Next part, under all three builds; Vite, Astro, axe and no theme flash are W5) and 6 (Firefox 129 floor verified; Safari 17.5 not tested; the free routes are in criterion 6). It partly advances 3: exports drift through `nz gen --check`, and the `.d.ts` import-graph walk from every `types` entry (`packages/react/test/built`). The committed raw export is one of criterion 12's R3 mitigations.
- **Moves to week 2:** `tokens.css` with `light-dark()`; the spring `linear()` transform; the `--nz-tracking-*` rename through `subValueVariableName` with an `em` transform; font-family tokens; the Lightning CSS build replacing the interim tsdown CSS copy; the tarball allowlist, publint/attw and the directive guard; a `defaultOpen` fixture page; Select Group and Separator coverage in the fixture. All of these landed in week 2.

**Week 2 status (2026-10-14).** 13 commits, each of which passes the full local gate (`pnpm check`) from a clean state; GitHub Actions stays off, so no CI run was involved. Built and verified:
- **Tokens:** the `@nizivo/tokens` build runs tsdown, `tz build` and the a11y gate, and writes `tokens.css` (one `light-dark()` declaration per semantic colour on `:root`, no `@property`), `custom-media.css`, `tailwind.css` and `contract.json`, each pinned by a byte golden. Typography is a `font` shorthand per style plus `--nz-tracking-*` in em; the font families are code-owned; the spring `linear()` pairs come from the one generator in the (mass, stiffness, damping, velocity) order the maintainer accepted (M12). The contrast matrix (`packages/tokens/CONTRAST.md`) has **58 checked pairs (29 per theme), all passing**, with **14 overrides and 19 proposals, all expiring 2027-01-31**, plus the drift, redundancy, expiry and metadata checks. The gate also runs uncached as `pnpm check:a11y` ([§15.4](#154-ownership-and-the-override-mechanism)).
- **CSS:** the Lightning CSS build (`packages/react/scripts/build-css.ts`) compiles **24 stylesheets** (23 components and `base.css`) to the browser floor, guards the output, and checks the emitted CSS against the vocabulary and the real `tokens.css` (425 token reads and 95 hooks, no missing name). **Every stylesheet is under the 2 KB gzip leaf target; the largest, `button.css`, is 1,013 bytes gzip** (`packages/react/W2-RESULTS.md`). `nz gen` writes `styles/base.css`: the a11y floor from `interactive`, `focusTarget` and `stateIndicator`, then `.nz-visually-hidden` in `nz.utilities`. `@nizivo/stylelint-config` carries the contract rules, `declaration-no-important` and `keyframes-name-pattern` ([§8.4](#84-component-css-hooks)).
- **Components:** the server-safe (T0) set: Text, Heading, Stack, Inline, Grid and GridItem, Container, Separator, Kbd, Image, Badge, Card, Delta, ProgressBar, ProgressRing, Meter, Skeleton, EmptyState, InlineError, and Alert with its `AlertDismiss` client leaf, one subpath each (D-h). `packages/react/test/ssr/t0.server.test.tsx` loads all **19 T0 entries** and renders their examples under React's `react-server` build, with Alert's dismiss leaf the only client module allowed; `render-all.test.tsx` renders all 27 examples against the vocabulary. Card has `padding` and `elevation`, Skeleton has `shape`, and the eight attributes behind them join the `presentational` group (D-j). Button renders no children when it has nothing to show ([§8.2](#82-class-slot-and-naming-conventions)).
- **Fixtures** (`fixtures/next/RESULTS.md`): all 13 Next pages pass under Turbopack, webpack, webpack + `optimizePackageImports` and `next dev`, with 0 console messages. `/t0` (subpaths) and `/t0-barrel` (root barrel) ship no `@nizivo` client module in any build, and `/alert-dismiss` ships only Alert's dismiss leaf. New pages also cover a Dialog and a Tooltip open on the server, Select groups and separator, and a server Button composed into `Select.Trigger`.
- **Floor lane on the real `tokens.css`** (`fixtures/floor-lane/RESULTS.md`, `results/w2-real-tokens/`): Firefox 129.0 passes every token-path check. Its 166 failures are all D1 probes that write `light-dark()` directly on the property, the same cells as in week 1. Chromium 156 and WebKit 27.2 have 0 failures. Safari 17.5 is still not tested.
- **Release checks:** `pnpm check:release` packs `@nizivo/tokens` and `@nizivo/react` and runs the tarball allowlist, publint (strict), attw (esm-only) and the directive guard, with its `export * as` lint, on the tarballs. It is the last step of `pnpm check`.
- **Figma:** the Grove → Nizivo rename and the `codeSyntax` rewrite were applied to the file (M10), and `Elevation/0 Flat` was fixed (M11).
- **Exit criteria:** advances 3: every check it names now exists and passes in the local gate (directive guard and `export * as` lint, emitted-CSS vocabulary check, Lightning CSS guard, exports drift, publint/attw, `.d.ts` walk); CI itself is off. Advances 7 in part: the matrix passes with the overrides; listing them on the Deviations page is W5. Advances 2 for the T0 set: the planted-marker rule holds for every T0 component through subpath and barrel, in Next only (Vite, Astro, axe and theme flash are W5). Advances 6: the floor lane runs on the shipped `tokens.css` (Firefox 129 verified; Safari 17.5 not tested). Not advanced: 14. Builders took Chromium forced-colours screenshots during review, but none is committed or reviewed by the accessibility owner, and the solid-outline check does not exist yet.
- **Moves:** the Fumadocs skeleton with generated tables goes to a separate small docs step right after week 2: a local static build only, with no Pages configuration and no workflow file while Actions is off (done: see the docs skeleton status below). `tokens.json` and the js-refs `.` entry of `@nizivo/tokens` (an empty module today) go to W3–4, before 0.1.0. A floor-lane page that renders the T0 component CSS on Firefox 129, Chromium and WebKit with computed-size checks, and forced-colours screenshots of the T0 set, go to W5 with visual regression and exit criterion 14. ProgressRing's `stroke` paint ([§8.5](#85-the-a11y-floor-nzbase)) goes to a follow-up. Safari 17.5 is still not tested. Also open: the `close` and `circle-x` glyphs with the Feather MIT notice ([§14.2](#142-icons-nizivoicons)); `ThemeScript` setting `data-nz-platform` before first paint (Kbd shows Ctrl spellings on Apple devices until then); READMEs for the published packages before 0.1.0; the rest of the `nz.utilities` classes; whether `AlertDismiss` keeps its export once `IconButton` exists, decided before 0.1.0 ([§9.5](#95-per-component-tier-table-core)).

**Docs skeleton status (2026-10-14).** The separate docs step after week 2 is built and passes the full local gate (`pnpm check`, which now ends with `pnpm docs:check`) from a clean state. Nothing is hosted. The measured numbers are in `apps/docs/RESULTS.md`.
- **Site:** `apps/docs` (`@nizivo/docs`, private): fumadocs-core and fumadocs-ui 16.17.2 and fumadocs-mdx 15.4.7 on Next 16.4.2 (Turbopack; 16.4.0 until 2026-10-22, GHSA-2464-fhr4-w9xw and GHSA-p3m5-59mp-jv5r), a static export (`output: 'export'`, `images: { unoptimized: true }`, trailing slashes) in `apps/docs/out`. 46 routes (Next's build counts 47 static pages): the home page, 41 docs pages (introduction, 4 getting started, 8 foundations, the components overview, 23 component pages, 3 utility entries and Deviations), a 404 page in the site's tokens, the static search index, `llms.txt` and `llms-full.txt`. The chrome is Fumadocs UI on Tailwind CSS 4.3.3, themed by Nizivo's tokens; Geist is self-hosted through `next/font/local`. An inline `<head>` script sets the `dark` class and `data-nz-theme` before `<body>` exists, standing in for `ThemeScript`.
- **Generated pages:** a new subpath gets its page with no hand edit. `apps/docs/scripts/generate.ts` reads the exports map and writes one route per component (D-n). Every table comes from `contracts/api`, `component.json`, `design/component-map.json`, `contract.json` or the examples; `apps/docs/content/components/<name>.mdx` adds optional prose and the keyboard map (Select, Tooltip and Dialog traced to react-aria 3.53.1). The Deviations page lists the 14 overrides with their expiry, the 19 proposals, D-i, the week 2 "builder decides" items each with the value the stylesheet ships (`apps/docs/lib/builder-choices.ts`; the build fails when an item has no recorded choice or the cited declaration has left its stylesheet), D1, D2 and the Safari 17.5 status. Plan citations such as §12.2 in the API records link to their section here.
- **API records:** `nz api` (`packages/tooling/api`; the format is `API-JSON.md`, the types `schema.ts`) writes `contracts/api/**`, 51 files, from the built packages (D-o). The root `pnpm gen:check` builds `@nizivo/react` and runs `nz api --check`.
- **Build and check:** `pnpm build` builds the site as the Turbo task `@nizivo/docs#build` (outputs `out/**`; its inputs add `contracts/api/**`, the examples, `component.json`, the code-owned token files and this plan, checked with `turbo --dry=json`; the `@nizivo/tokens` build output reaches its hash through `dependsOn: ["^build"]`); `@nizivo/docs#typecheck` caches the files it generates (`.source`, `.generated`, the component routes, `next-env.d.ts`, `.next/types`), because the type-aware lint reads them. `pnpm docs:check` serves `out/` and runs `apps/docs/scripts/smoke.ts` in Playwright Chromium: every page answers 200, logs no console error or warning, hydrates, makes no request outside the local server and has 0 axe violations (WCAG 2.2 A/AA tags) in light; dark is reported, and is also 0. axe's best-practice rules are reported. It also checks, at 375 px in the dark pass, that no table, code block or preview is cut off without a scroller (WCAG 1.4.10; every generated table sits in a focusable, named scroller), that no raw Markdown shows outside code, the 404 status, which Nizivo client modules each page loads, the theme switch (Light, Dark and System) and the no-flash script, nested and inverse scopes, the Tailwind `nz` utilities, Select, Tooltip and Dialog by keyboard, a selected Select option in forced colours, the skip link, the mobile drawer (modal while open: the page is inert, Escape closes it and focus returns), and offline search, which returns focus to its button on Escape. Turbo's global dependencies now name `contracts/layers.css` and `contracts/vocabulary.json` instead of `contracts/**`, so an `nz api` run no longer invalidates every cache.
- **Performance and offline:** the JavaScript on every page is Next, React and Fumadocs (199 KiB gzip on the home page, 215 KiB on the server-only Badge page). A Nizivo client module loads only where an example renders one, and only its own: Alert's dismiss leaf, Tooltip (233 KiB), Dialog (273 KiB) and Select (283 KiB, with React Aria). The search engine and its index load on the first search. A cold build passes with outbound network denied by `sandbox-exec`, so the build makes no network call.
- **Exit criteria:** advances 10 (every page it names exists and builds; nothing is live), 7 (every override is on the Deviations page with its expiry, locally) and 9 in part (the `contracts/api` manifests exist).
- **Deferred:** hosting (the Pages configuration, the deploy workflow and the DNS `CNAME` record at neevtechnologies.in) waits for the maintainer and for Actions; the maintainer adds the DNS record later, before the hosting step (2026-10-15). "Open in StackBlitz" waits too, because it calls an external service ([§18.2](#182-docs-site-public)). Storybook ([§18.3](#183-storybook-internal-workshop)) is not started. The Vite and Astro pages stay drafts until their W5 fixtures. The docs checks run in Chromium only: Firefox 129 and Safari 17.5 are not run, forced colours is checked for Select's selected option only, and reduced motion was checked once by hand (the Fumadocs drawer and dialogs drop their animations). `ci.yml` does not run `docs:check` yet. Unstable entries get a page section once one exists, and `ThemeScript` replaces the inline script when it ships.

**Week 3 status (2026-10-14).** The first half of W3–4: the Field system and form controls, NativeSelect and IconButton, built by three builders on a shared Field contract and integrated into one tree that passes the full local gate (`pnpm check`) from a clean state with `TURBO_FORCE=true` (142 s). GitHub Actions stays off. Measured numbers: `packages/react/W3-RESULTS.md`. **Review pass (2026-10-15):** a three-reviewer pass (a11y and forms, design and CSS, architecture and SSR) raised 23 findings; the fixes (focus after the inline error renders, SegmentedControl's single Tab stop in Chromium, the forced-colours Switch thumb, NativeSelect wired when placed directly in a Field, `--nz-focus-halo-critical`, `aria-keyshortcuts`, nz api roots through builders) and the open items (D-q, D-aa, D-ab) are in W3-RESULTS.md, and the gate passes again from clean (128 s) before the week is committed.
- **Components (11 new subpaths, 34 in all):** `field` (Field, `useFieldControl`) and `fieldset` (D-r); `input`, `textarea`, `checkbox`, `radio-group` (RadioGroup, Radio), `switch` and `segmented-control`, each a thin client leaf over native inputs with no engine (D-t, D-u); `native-select` (server, D-x); `icon-button` (server shell, default tooltip leaf, D-v); `theme-script` (`data-nz-platform` only, D-z). Field is a server shell that hands serialisable props to one client leaf, FieldScope, which owns the context, native validation messages and the whole anatomy; the function child is the zero-JS path (D-q). Controls follow `packages/react/src/field/FIELD-CONTRACT.md`. Tooltip gained `content` and `shortcut` (D-d), and its skin now follows the Figma master. Textarea grows with its content where `field-sizing` is supported (D-w).
- **Glyphs and licences** ([§14.2](#142-icons-nizivoicons)): `circle-x`, `close`, `check` and `minus` are inlined, all four Feather-derived; `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES` carries the Feather MIT notice and `notices.test.ts` checks every glyph by its Lucide name. `InlineError`'s `icon` and `AlertDismiss`'s `children` default to them; `AlertDismiss` keeps its export (decided before 0.1.0).
- **Generated and contracts:** `nz gen` wrote the exports, barrels, `base.css` (focus, `touch-action` and forced-colours rules for every new interactive slot and state indicator) and 10 new `design/component-map.json` entries; the `nested-input` focus rule now also matches `[data-focus-visible]` ([§8.5](#85-the-a11y-floor-nzbase)). `nz api` wrote the records for every new subpath. The Lightning CSS guard accepts one property beyond the floor, `field-sizing`, and only inside `@supports (field-sizing: …)` (`SUPPORTS_GATED_PROPERTIES`). The vocabulary needed no new attribute; `data-orientation` and `data-focus-visible` name their new users.
- **Contrast:** 62 checked pairs (31 per theme) pass, with the same 14 overrides and 19 proposals; the new pairs cover the checked fills, the Switch tracks and thumb, a control border in a selected row and the pressed IconButton edge. No Figma colour failed.
- **Server safety:** `t0.server.test.tsx` now covers every `tier: "server"` manifest (25), on the shared RSC helper that replaces client leaves with client references and checks that every prop crossing into one serialises; each client-tier control proves the same in its own test file. A race in that helper (the mocked React not yet in place, about one run in three) is fixed for every test.
- **Next fixture** (`fixtures/next/RESULTS.md`): 18 of 18 pages pass under Turbopack, webpack, webpack + `optimizePackageImports` and `next dev`, with 0 console messages. A server form wires every Field in the server HTML, validates inline after hydration, and submits natively with JavaScript off; NativeSelect alone ships no client module; IconButton's tooltip shows on focus without describing the button; `ThemeScript` in `<head>` hydrates cleanly. **Measured:** a leaf that a server shell imports but the page does not render (FieldScope behind a function child, IconButton's tooltip behind `tooltip={false}`) runs no code anywhere, but Turbopack and `next dev` still ship it; production webpack does not ([§9.5](#95-per-component-tier-table-core), [§16.1](#161-forms-gap-report-a-d16)).
- **Docs:** 53 pages pass `pnpm docs:check` (no console message, axe WCAG 2.2 A/AA clean in light and dark, 375 px reflow, and each page loads only the Nizivo client modules its examples import). Each new component has a generated page with its examples, tables and an Accessibility section whose keyboard map comes from the native element or React Aria. The Deviations page lists all 29 week 3 values Figma leaves to the builder ([figma/12 §5](figma/12-w3-forms.md)), each checked against the declaration that ships it, and the docs site's head script now ends with `themeScript`.
- **Floor lane** (`fixtures/floor-lane/RESULTS.md`): a new page on the shipped CSS passes 20 of 20 checks in both OS schemes on Firefox 129.0, Chromium 156 and WebKit 27.2: `field-sizing` behind `@supports` (Firefox 129 keeps `rows`), `:has()` inside `:is()`, `1lh`, `:dir(rtl)` with `translate`, `:checked` and `:indeterminate` on `appearance: none` inputs, and the nested-input focus rule. Safari 17.5 is still not tested.
- **Browser lane:** `pnpm --filter @nizivo/react test:browser` (Vitest browser mode, Chromium) runs 57 hydration, validation, keyboard and geometry tests; it is not part of `pnpm check`. Builders also ran it in WebKit; Playwright's Firefox does not launch on this machine.
- **Exit criteria:** advances 2 for the week 3 set in Next only (zero console messages and zero hydration warnings in all four modes; the server-only NativeSelect page ships no client chunk; Vite, Astro, axe in the fixture and no theme flash stay W5). Keeps 3 green with every new subpath, and extends the Lightning CSS guard. Advances 4 in part: axe is clean on every new docs page in light and dark, but there are no stories yet. Advances 6: Firefox 129 passes the week 3 CSS; Safari 17.5 not tested. Advances 7: the matrix passes with the overrides, and the Deviations page now lists the week 3 builder values too. Advances 9 in part: `contracts/api` covers all 34 subpaths; `size-limit` is W5, and W3-RESULTS.md is its baseline. Advances 10 in part: every new page builds locally; nothing is live. Advances 14 in part: the browser lane asserts a solid outline on Input, Textarea, NativeSelect and a SegmentedControl item, and the floor page on a marked item; Checkbox, Radio, Switch and IconButton were checked only in builders' scratch runs, and no forced-colours screenshot is committed or reviewed. Not advanced: 1, 5 (conformance is week 4), 8, 11, 12, 13.
- **Moves to week 4:** besides the planned second half (the React Aria `OverlayEngine` and `engineDom` conformance for Tooltip, Dialog and Select, then Menu, Tabs and TabNav, `@nizivo/icons`, `tokens.json` and the js-refs `.` entry): Select's Field wiring with `labelBehavior: 'focus'`, and a `select.css` pass to match Input and NativeSelect (sm radius, disabled border, an invalid border); `Dialog.Close` defaulting to an IconButton with the close glyph. Open, with no week yet: whether Field and IconButton get a separate entry with no client import, so the zero-JS paths ship zero bytes under Turbopack too (a maintainer decision); clearing a native message on `form.reset()`; an inline native message for a required RadioGroup and for checkbox groups; Switch `pending`; the Textarea `count`; a description line for Checkbox and Switch rows; sizes for the selection controls; the theme half of `ThemeScript`; forced-colours screenshots and the outline check for every control (W5, exit criterion 14); Firefox in the browser lane; Safari 17.5.

**Week 4 status (2026-10-15).** The second half of W3–4: `@nizivo/icons`, `tokens.json` and the typed `.` entry of `@nizivo/tokens`, the overlay conformance suite for Tooltip, Dialog and Select, then Menu, Tabs and TabNav, and the Safari 17.5 floor lane on the iOS 17.5 Simulator. Built by five builders and integrated into one tree that passes the full local gate (`pnpm check`) from a clean state with `TURBO_FORCE=true` (158 s, rerun after the review fixes: 158 s). GitHub Actions stays off; the work is committed locally after the week 4 review, in one commit per area, and nothing is pushed. The maintainer's 2026-10-15 answers are M13 to M16 ([§23.3](#233-maintainer-decisions-2026-10-14)). Measured numbers: `packages/react/W4-RESULTS.md`.
- **Components (3 new subpaths, 37 in all; every v0.1 component in the scope list now exists):** `menu` (Root, Trigger, Popup, Item, CheckboxItem, RadioGroup, RadioItem, Group, GroupLabel, Separator; tone `critical`, `shortcut`, `icon`, link items through `href` or `render`; D-ag), `tabs` (Tabs, engine) and `tab-nav` (TabNav, server, 0 KB; D-ac). Menu has no `SubmenuTrigger` (D-ag; the maintainer decides). New stylesheets are 857 to 1,025 B gzip; `base.css` is 1,017 B.
- **Overlay conformance** (`packages/react/test/overlay`, `pnpm --filter @nizivo/react test:overlay`, not in `pnpm check`): one suite runs against Tooltip, Dialog, Select and Menu, plus nesting and Menu cases. **Chromium: 82 passed, 7 skipped; WebKit (Playwright 27.2): 77 passed, 12 skipped** (after the review's cases) (skips are cases that do not apply to a subject, and the Chromium-only forced-colours case). It covers open by pointer and every documented key, Escape and focus return, outside press, the one scroll lock with `<html>` and `<body>` restored, portal and `container`, the one overlay band, vocabulary-only parts, entry and exit through `data-entering` and `data-exiting`, reduced motion, the spring pair, `defaultOpen` hydration, Strict Mode cycles (O-10), and O-01, O-03, O-04 and O-22 (Esc stack). It found five gaps in the shipped overlays, all fixed (D-af): Tab trapped in Select's popup, Dialog focusing itself instead of its first field, a Tooltip inside a Dialog inert, focus left on `<body>` after a server-open popup closed, and Select's fade cut to 0 ms under reduced motion (it now enters on the spring pair like the others). The `OverlayEngine` interface in `_internal/overlay.ts` is explicit, and the node lane checks that no contract reaches the engine. Not covered: Firefox (Playwright's Firefox does not launch here), O-02, O-11 beyond the trigger step.
- **`@nizivo/icons`** (D-ad): 1,879 Lucide icons from `lucide-static` 1.56.0 and the Instagram glyph from `simple-icons` 16.35.0, one server-safe module each, built by a generator with no committed output except `manifest.json` and `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES`. The tarball is 3,768 files, 305,566 bytes packed; one icon from the barrel is 502 B gzip in a minified bundle, each further icon 71 B. `check:release` packs it with its own allowlist (publint, attw, the directive guard). The docs site has a searchable Icons page, rendered on the server from a build-time sprite.
- **Tokens** (D-ae): `dist/tokens.json` (237 tokens, flat, with provenance per context) and `dist/index.js` / `index.d.ts` (one `var()` reference group per export, 2,394 bytes gzip in all) are byte-golden and checked against the built `tokens.css` (2,338 comparisons); the four W2 outputs are unchanged. Docs page "Tokens in JavaScript".
- **Contrast:** still 62 checked rows, 31 per theme, all passing, with the same 14 overrides and 19 proposals. New cells: `text-tertiary` on `bg-brand-subtle` (light 5.71, dark 6.67; a checked Menu item keeps its shortcut) and, reported only, `bg-hover` and `bg-brand-subtle` on `bg-surface-raised` (the Menu and Select highlight and the checked fill: 1.07 to 1.16, so the focus indicator is the generated outline) and `text-disabled` on `bg-brand-subtle`. Use notes name the Menu, Tabs and TabNav users of existing rows.
- **Generated and contracts:** `nz gen` wrote the three subpaths, `base.css` (focus and `touch-action` for the menu items and trigger, the tab and the TabNav link; forced-colours rules for the Menu popup, checked items and the two indicators) and four `design/component-map.json` entries (Menu `7275:11516` and ghost `746:203`, Tabs `7275:14781` and ghost `755:961`); `nz api` wrote six records (78 files in all). A lint now bans `@nizivo/icons` and `lucide*` imports in `packages/react/src`.
- **Server safety:** `t0.server.test.tsx` covers TabNav (26 server manifests); Tabs proves its RSC boundary in its own test (`test/ssr/tabs.test.tsx`), and Menu, like Select and Dialog, through the Next fixture. **Next fixture** (`fixtures/next/RESULTS.md`): **23 of 23 pages pass under Turbopack, webpack, webpack + `optimizePackageImports` and `next dev`, 0 console messages, no build warnings.** New: a Menu open on the server whose Escape returns focus to the trigger; Tabs whose selected panel (only) is in the server HTML, wired both ways; TabNav through `next/link` with no `@nizivo` client module; icons from the barrel in a Server Component (no icon module ships) and in a client component (exactly the three imported icons and the shared base ship, in every mode, plain webpack included).
- **Floor lane** (`fixtures/floor-lane/RESULTS.md`): **Mobile Safari 17.5 on the iOS 17.5 Simulator** (`run.ts --ios-sim`) passes every check on the plan sample, the built `tokens.css` and the week 3 controls, 0 failures, with one new degradation, **D4** (an exit to `display: none` is instant, as D2), and D3 confirmed; both are on the Deviations page and in §18.2. Rerun on the final tree, Firefox 129 and Mobile Safari 17.5 give cell-for-cell the same results as the committed files. macOS Safari 17.5 is not tested.
- **Docs:** 58 pages pass `pnpm docs:check` (axe WCAG 2.2 A/AA clean in light and dark, 375 px reflow, each page loading only its examples' client modules). New pages: Menu, Tabs, TabNav, Icons and Tokens in JavaScript. The Deviations page lists the 22 week 4 builder values of figma/13 §5, each checked against the file that ships it, and D1 to D4 with both Safari 17.5 status lines. Tooltip pages stayed at 234 KB gzip of JavaScript once the modal-popup helpers moved out of `overlay.client.tsx` (D-af); Menu loads 277 KB, Tabs 255 KB, TabNav and Icons 215 to 216 KB (no Nizivo client module).
- **Exit criteria:** advances 2 for Menu, Tabs, TabNav and the icons in Next only (all four modes; TabNav and server-rendered icons ship no client chunk; Vite, Astro, axe in the fixture and no theme flash stay W5). Keeps 3 green, now with `@nizivo/icons` in `check:release` (attw does not expand its wildcard subpaths; a scratch consumer typechecked them under `bundler` and `node16`). Advances 4 in part (axe on the new docs pages; no stories). **Advances 5:** the conformance suite exists and passes for Select, Dialog, Tooltip and Menu in Chromium and WebKit, with the v0.1 O-tests O-01, O-03, O-04, O-10, O-17 (SSR) and O-22, O-20 (exit through `data-exiting`) in two engines and O-21 (forced colours) by computed style in Chromium; Tabs has its own browser spec (Chromium 10 of 10; WebKit 8 of 10, the two failing steps Tab onto a link, which WebKit on macOS skips by default); O-11 is only partly covered, Firefox is not run, and the lane is local, not CI. **Advances 6:** the floor lane now runs Safari 17.5 for iOS and passes with D3 and D4 documented; with Firefox 129 already verified, only macOS Safari 17.5 is open, recorded as not tested on the Deviations page as the criterion allows. Advances 7 (the matrix passes; the Deviations page lists the week 4 builder values). Advances 9 in part (`contracts/api` covers all 37 subpaths; `size-limit` is W5). Advances 10 in part (new pages build locally; nothing is live). Advances 14 in part (the overlay lane asserts a solid outline on Menu items and the Tabs spec on tabs and TabNav links; forced colours by computed style only; no screenshot reviewed). Not advanced: 1, 8, 11, 12, 13.
- **Week 4 review** (three reviewers, 24 findings; `packages/react/W4-RESULTS.md` "Week 4 review"). Fixed: the pointer drew the keyboard ring on Menu and Select rows in Chromium, because React Aria moves DOM focus to the hovered row; a new `focusTarget` mode, `engine`, follows React Aria's `data-focus-visible` only and turns the native ring off. In forced colours a checked row's inset ring and glyphs kept their token colours (`forced-color-adjust` is inherited); the `highlight` paint now sets a system outline colour and disabled Menu and Select rows are `GrayText`. The open Select listbox failed axe `aria-required-children` (a `role="separator"` child); the separator is presentational. A hovered checked Menu row changes fill; mixed menus line up their shortcuts; `--nz-menu-border`; an empty icon `label` names nothing; the Tabs label tracking; the Icons page tiles; Simulator cleanup on every exit; `OverlayEngine.completes` (§12.2); one 0.1.0 changeset. **Deferred:** Tabs arrow keys follow NizivoConfig's `locale`, not a `dir="rtl"` attribute, so a page that sets only `dir` gets reversed arrows; reading the direction from the DOM after hydration, or requiring the locale for RTL with a dev warning, is the maintainer's call (documented on the Tabs and Deviations pages). (Resolved in week 7 by M26: the scope's locale, overridden by `dir` on `Tabs.Root`.) An overflow cue for a scrolling tab strip, and hiding the classic scrollbar, need techniques outside the Safari 17.5 floor (listed on the Deviations page). Real Lucide glyphs in the Menu and Tabs examples wait for a docs-generator route, because L6's lint bans icon imports in `packages/react` and copied path data would need a Lucide notice there. An open-state axe pass for the overlays (the docs smoke runs axe on closed popups only). Inline icons in running text need `vertical-align: -0.125em`, documented rather than shipped on every icon.
- **Moves to W5:** Select's Field wiring with `labelBehavior: 'focus'`, the `select.css` visual pass (figma/13 §4.3: radius, elevation and border, row gaps, the brand-subtle selected look, group label; it should share Menu's item recipe) and `Dialog.Close` defaulting to an IconButton, all three planned for week 4 and not built; O-02 and the rest of O-11 (`returnFocus`, the parent-trigger step, the dev warning); the submenu decision (D-ag); Firefox in the browser and overlay lanes; macOS Safari 17.5; wiring `test:overlay` into a gate once Actions returns; Turbopack and `next dev` build time for the icons barrel (not measured); search by Lucide tags on the Icons page; and the planned W5 work (Vite and Astro fixtures, `size-limit`, visual regression, the local release script, READMEs for `@nizivo/react` and `@nizivo/tokens`). Still open for the maintainer: D-q, D-aa and the `AlertDismiss` export.

**Week 5 status (2026-10-15).** The planned W5 work and the items carried into it, built by six builders (one preparing the dependencies) and integrated into one tree. `pnpm check` passes from a clean state with `TURBO_FORCE=true` (**160 s**), and the slow lanes of the new `pnpm check:full` pass after it (284 s). GitHub Actions stays off; the work is committed locally after the week 5 review, in one commit per area, and nothing is pushed, tagged or published. Measured numbers: `packages/react/W5-RESULTS.md`.
- **Gate split** (D-ak): `pnpm check` gains `pnpm size` and `pnpm floor:check`; `pnpm check:full` adds the browser lane, overlay conformance in Chromium and WebKit, the three fixtures and visual regression, the list the release script runs (`packages/tooling/release/lanes.ts`). The floor lanes stay manual. Overlay conformance in Firefox is in neither: Playwright's Firefox still does not launch on this machine. New root scripts: `size`, `floor`, `floor:gen`, `floor:check`, `test:browser`, `test:overlay`, `test:visual`, `fixture:next`, `fixture:vite`, `fixture:astro`, `check:full`, `release`.
- **Fixtures:** Vite 8.3.4 (`@vitejs/plugin-react` 6.1.2) and Astro 7.3.8 (`@astrojs/react` 7.0.1), each its own pnpm workspace root on the packed packages with exact inline pins, no install script and a committed lockfile, run the shared spec with Next. **Next 23 of 23 pages in all four modes, Vite 12 of 12, Astro 12 of 12; 0 console messages, 0 axe violations (axe-core 4.14.0, WCAG 2.0–2.2 A/AA, light), no build warnings, all 68 `light-dark()` values kept, and no theme flash in any build**, with the late-theme negative control caught. Found: a plain Vite 8 build lowers every `light-dark()` (`build.cssTarget` at the floor fixes it; a browserslist does not); Vite 8.3.4 prints no `MODULE_LEVEL_DIRECTIVE` warning and tree-shakes the barrel completely; Astro per-island ids hold at runtime, server-only pages load no script, compound parts as `.astro` children fail the build, and Astro 7 backgrounds `astro dev`/`preview` under a coding agent (`--ignore-lock`). `ThemeScript` does not prevent a flash (it sets only `data-nz-platform`); `data-nz-theme` on `<html>` and the app's own stored-choice script do. §11.1 records each.
- **Size budgets and seam cost** (D-ah, `packages/react/SIZES.md`): 118 size-limit checks over every public entry of the three packages, the own code of the 23 client modules and the K6 and K7 helpers, at measured + 10% capped at §17.1's limits (after the review), inside `pnpm check` (about 1 s). Every engine subpath is under its §17.1 limit (Select 62,404 B against 66 KiB; Tooltip 16,327 B against 16 KiB); their React Aria part is at or under r3. K6 measures 757 B (estimate ≤ 0.5 KB) and K7 678 B (~0.4 KB), recorded in §4.2. Nizivo costs +1.6 KB (Tabs, Tooltip) to +4.5 KB (Menu) gzip over a hand-written React Aria layer with the same parts.
- **Visual regression** (D-ai): 864 baselines of 74 examples in light, dark and **forced colours**, at rest, hovered, focused and open; four compare runs on the final tree report no difference. (After the review: 1,496 baselines, with forced colours on a dark OS and the pressed state added, at the shipped 16 px root, under an exact compare; see the week 5 review below.) It found, and the integration fixed, a forced-colours Menu defect (a checked row under the pointer or keyboard lost its label to the hover token, and the keyboard ring around a checked row was invisible) and the browser underline on a Button rendered as a link. The design sign-off set (43 light renders of Button, Input, Select and Dialog) is generated by `pnpm test:visual --review`.
- **Floor lane, every v0.1 component's CSS:** `fixtures/floor-lane/gen-w5.ts` renders every component from the built `dist` (open popups composed from the engine's DOM shape) into three pages with computed-size and painted checks; Firefox 129.0, Chromium 156, WebKit 27.2 and Mobile Safari 17.5 each pass **265 of 265 per scheme** (269 after the review). No new degradation: D1 to D4 stand. One component finding, F1 (Menu rows 48 px instead of 36 px without a `border-box` reset, against §8.1), was fixed at integration; the review fixed the same reset dependence in the Menu popup, the Tooltip, the Dialog popup and a Button rendered as a link.
- **Release script** (D-aj, M14): `pnpm release`, a dry run by default, and `pnpm release --publish`; the procedure is in `RELEASING.md` and `CONTRIBUTING.md`. The full dry run on the integrated tree (449 s) passes the gate, every lane, pack, `check:release`, the licence gate and `npm publish --dry-run` for all three packages in order, and stops on three honest blockers: an uncommitted tree, no npm login, and package folders that differ from HEAD. **READMEs** now ship in all three packages, and `check:release` requires one.
- **Docs:** the Deviations page also lists every §23.4 builder default (37 rows, generated from the table), and the smoke test fails if any degradation of §18.2 or any §23.4 row is missing. Week 5 added no Figma value to choose. The Vite and Astro getting-started pages lose their draft notes and match their fixtures (the Vite page gains the `build.cssTarget` step); the Next page's two stale fixture notes are corrected. 58 pages pass `pnpm docs:check`.
- **Exit criteria:** **advances 2 to done for the stated spec:** Next (Turbopack and webpack), Vite 8 and Astro 7.3 install the packed packages and pass the shared spec, with axe and no theme flash added in all three (Chromium only; Firefox and WebKit fixture runs wait for CI). Keeps 3 green. Advances 6: the component CSS of every v0.1 component passes on Firefox 129 and Mobile Safari 17.5 with no new degradation; **macOS Safari 17.5 is still not tested**, as the Deviations page says. Keeps 7 (the page lists every override, builder value and builder default). **Advances 9 to done locally:** budgets at measured + 10% are green inside `pnpm check`, the seam overhead is measured and recorded, and the `contracts/api` manifests are committed. Advances 10 in part (the getting-started pages are verified against fixtures; nothing is live). **Advances 11 and 14 to review-ready:** the sign-off set and the forced-colours shots of every v0.1 control in every state exist (748 after the review, in a light and a dark OS palette, pressed included), but neither the designer nor the accessibility owner has reviewed them, and the solid-outline Playwright check covers inputs, Menu items, tabs and TabNav links, not every T0 control. Advances 1 in part: the release script exists and its dry run passes every step it can on an uncommitted tree; the publish itself is W6. Not advanced: 4 (no stories), 5 (Firefox not run), 8, 12, 13.
- **Plan corrections folded in:** §11.1 (no `styles.css` in the package yet: the import rule is `tokens.css`, `base.css` and one `css/<name>.css` per component; the Vite CSS target; no Vite directive warning; the Next browserslist now in the fixture; Astro's agent behaviour and runtime-verified island ids), §18.1 (visual regression as built; `check:release` packs all three packages and requires a README), §19.2 (the script's path and order), §6 (new folders), §17 and §4.2 (W5 numbers).
- **Week 5 review** (three reviewers: fixtures and accessibility; visual and floor; release, build and plan; 21 findings; `packages/react/W5-RESULTS.md` "Week 5 review"). 19 were fixed before the week 5 commits (two of them in part), 2 are deferred with a reason, none was rejected. Fixed: **reset independence (§8.1) beyond F1:** `.nz-button`, `.nz-menu-popup`, `.nz-tooltip-popup` and `.nz-dialog-popup` set `box-sizing: border-box`, so a Button rendered as a link (`render={<a />}`) is 40 px like its `<button>` siblings (it was 42, and `fullWidth` overflowed its container), the Menu popup is exactly its trigger's width, and a long Tooltip is 320 px, not 344; a Dialog popup that holds focus itself shows no browser ring (`outline: none`, as the Menu and Select lists). The floor lane asserts the outer boxes, derives text heights from the type tokens, adds link-Button specimens and commits each check's observed value: **269 of 269 per scheme** on Firefox 129.0, Chromium 156, WebKit 27.2 and Mobile Safari 17.5. **Visual regression:** the harness had put `body-m` on `<html>`, so every rem rendered at 87.5% (a 35 px Button); the root now stays at 16 px. The compare is exact to ±2 per channel with 3 stray pixels per shot (YIQ 0.1 had let a one-step `bg-brand` or `border-default` change through; both now fail, as does a 1 px radius change), and `--update` re-records exactly what a compare fails. Forced colours with a dark OS and the pressed state were added, and the Button example shows `accent`, `lg` and an enabled `critical`: **1,496 baselines** (4.7 MB; 748 in forced colours), recorded fresh. **Fixtures:** axe also runs with each interaction-opened popup open and after a server-open overlay closes (both blocking), and once with the OS dark (`axeDark`, reported, exit criterion 4): 0 violations in every state, light and dark, in all three fixtures. Any build warning now fails a run. The runners write results to a temporary folder unless given `--record`, so `pnpm check:full` and the release script leave the tree clean, and the release script blocks if the gate or a lane changes a file. **Release script:** a version already on the registry with other bytes than the checked tarball stops a real run before anything is published or tagged (`RELEASING.md` step 7). **Size:** budgets are measured + 10% capped at the §17.1 limits; the stale `component.json` `budget` fields are gone. **Docs:** the Next page's theme snippet is now the pattern the fixture checks (`data-nz-theme="system"`, the `nz-theme` stored-choice script, `ThemeScript`); two Astro island bullets say the fixture does not check them; the Vite byte comparison no longer names a figure that drifts; the `@nizivo/react` README gives the Vite `build.cssTarget` step. **Deferred:** iOS Safari zooms into a focused text field under 16 px, and Input, Textarea, NativeSelect and Select use 14 px Body/M with no coarse-pointer rule (a design decision: 16 px on coarse pointers, or a Deviations entry; the floor check follows the decision); the npm 2FA mode of the publishing account, which needs a logged-in `npm profile get --json` before the first real publish (an empty one-time-password answer lets npm prompt itself if the shape differs). **In part:** the pressed state of popup items and a forced-colours hover cue on Select and Menu rows stay with the accessibility owner; a `Dialog.Close` flush with the popup edge has its ring clipped by the content's `overflow: clip` (folded into the W6 `Dialog.Close`-as-IconButton work). Also found: after keyboard use, pressing a Tooltip trigger with no pointer move first leaves an unpositioned tooltip in `data-exiting` (React Aria 1.22.1; the visual lane moves the pointer first). On the committed tree, `pnpm check` passes from clean with `TURBO_FORCE=true` (the tooling tests now have a 120 s timeout: two that spawn node processes ran past vitest's 5 s default, and past 30 s, on a loaded machine), and `pnpm release` passes every step, leaves the tree clean and stops only on an unpushed HEAD and no npm login.
- **W6 needs:** *Hardening:* Select's Field wiring with `labelBehavior: 'focus'`, the `select.css` visual pass and `Dialog.Close` defaulting to an IconButton (planned for week 4, moved to W5, not built in W5); O-02 and the rest of O-11; a minimum target size for a bare `Dialog.Trigger` or `Tooltip.Trigger`, or docs that always compose a Button (two side by side fail WCAG 2.2 2.5.8; reported, not decided); a forced-colours hover cue on unselected Select and Menu rows (for the accessibility owner); the solid-outline check on every T0 control; a decision on 16 px text fields under a coarse pointer (iOS Safari zooms into smaller ones), then a floor check that follows it; a ring for a `Dialog.Close` flush with the popup edge; the Tooltip left in `data-exiting` after a keyboard-then-press sequence; axe in forced colours and reduced motion in the fixtures (dark is reported since the review); a Vite dev-server flash check; Firefox in the browser and overlay lanes; Linux visual baselines once Docker or CI exists; the `ThemeScript` theme half and `styles.css`. *Reviews:* the designer signs off the light renders (exit criterion 11) and the accessibility owner reviews the forced-colours shots (exit criterion 14). *The maintainer's decisions, still open:* D-q, D-aa, the `AlertDismiss` export, D-ag (submenus), Tabs direction from `dir="rtl"` (A11Y-4), the tab-strip overflow cue against the floor, plus three size questions reported in week 5: whether the dashboard set's "75 KB" is KiB (74.4 passes) or decimal kB (76.2 fails), K6 at 757 B against its estimate, and Field at 3,136 B against the 2.5 KB target. *Publish:* push the week 5 commits, run `cd ~ && npm profile get --json` (superseded by M30: see RELEASING.md "The npm login") once logged in and pin the 2FA shape the release script expects, then publish 0.1.0 on `next` by hand through `pnpm release --publish` (exit criterion 1), and record whether npm also set `latest`. Still open from earlier weeks: the designer's TokensBrücke plugin export (exit criterion 8) and macOS Safari 17.5.

**Week 6 status (2026-10-15).** Hardening, built by four builders (Select; overlays; `ThemeScript` and `styles.css`; test lanes) and integrated into one tree. GitHub Actions stays off; nothing is committed, pushed, tagged or published by the integration. Measured numbers: `packages/react/W6-RESULTS.md`. **The gate was red at integration:** `@nizivo/react/dialog` (32,121 B after the review) and the dashboard set (77,260 B) were over their §17.1 limits by 377 B and 460 B, so `pnpm size` and the budgets unit test failed while every later step of `pnpm check` passed. **M17 (2026-10-16): the maintainer raised both limits by 1 KiB** (Dialog 32 KiB, the dashboard set 76 KiB), in a commit placed before the overlays commit, so every week 6 commit passes `pnpm check`.
- **Select** (D-aq): wired to its Field (`useFieldControl({ labelBehavior: 'focus' })`: the Field's id, name, flags and description wiring; a label click focuses the trigger with its ring and never opens the list; the value reaches `FormData` through the hidden native `<select>`), and the `select.css` pass of figma/13 §4.3: the trigger matches NativeSelect in every state, and the popup and rows share Menu's recipe (`--nz-radius-lg`, border, `--nz-elevation-3`, 36 px rows 2 px apart, the brand-subtle selected row with a trailing check and an `indicator` prop, Menu's group label and separator, the inset ring; since M36, 2026-10-18, the popup follows Figma's Select Menu 7356:406 and its Option, Group Label and Divider instead, keeping the tint, check and inset ring).
- **Overlays:** `Dialog.Close` with no `render` or `children` is an IconButton with the close glyph, in the popup's top-end corner when it is a direct child of `Dialog.Popup`; a control flush with the content edge draws its ring inside, so the content's corner clip no longer cuts it (D-an). O-11 is complete (D-ao): `returnFocus` on `Dialog.Root`, the parent-overlay step and a development-only warning; O-02 already passes. The Tooltip left in `data-exiting` after keyboard use and a press with no pointer move is reproduced, root-caused in React Aria 1.22.1 (an instant close during warm-up leaves the exit state stuck) and fixed in the adapter (D-ap).
- **`ThemeScript` theme half** (D-al, [§7.4](#74-theme-mechanism)): a stored `nz-theme` of `light`, `dark` or `system` replaces the server's `data-nz-theme` before first paint; 379 bytes, `sha256-nA1HUQWDdDUFqqoxWbjzCzcYSjzWLEe0pwDdRCMGqOw=`. The docs site renders `ThemeScript` instead of its own script, and the fixtures dropped theirs, so their `stored` theme-flash case tests `ThemeScript`.
- **`@nizivo/react/styles.css`** (D-am, [§5.2](#52-exports-maps)): tokens, base and every stable component in one file, an exact concatenation with a byte-identical `tokens.css`; exported, packed by `check:release` (react: 254 files), measured (14,882 B gzip, budget 16 KiB). Not yet imported by any fixture or the docs site, so its behaviour through a bundler (the 68 `light-dark()` values, the layer order) is unchecked.
- **Test lanes:** a solid-outline check on every T0 control in the browser lane (Chromium 30 of 30 with forced colours; Firefox and WebKit 15 each). It found that a focused checked Checkbox, Radio or Switch kept its ring in the token green in forced colours; the generator's `fill` paint now sets `outline-color: CanvasText` (D-at). axe now runs in forced colours and with reduced motion in all three fixtures (0 violations; a documented text-fill workaround for a Chromium + axe false positive, D-as), and a Vite dev-server theme check reports the 3 frames Vite paints before its JavaScript-injected styles (a dev-only effect; documented). Firefox runs: Playwright's Firefox needed a home folder of its own on macOS (`CFFIXED_USER_HOME`); the browser lane (89 passed, 15 skipped) and the overlay suite (109 passed, 13 skipped, three runs) pass in it, and both joined `RELEASE_LANES` (D-ar).

Next (all four modes), Vite and Astro pass on a fresh pack and were recorded with `--record`: 0 failures, 0 console messages, 0 build warnings, 0 axe violations in light, dark, forced colours and reduced motion, 68 of 68 `light-dark()` values kept, no theme flash. The floor lane on the new CSS: Firefox 129.0, Chromium 156, WebKit 27.2 and Mobile Safari 17.5 pass 276 of 276 per scheme (the composed Select popup now carries the check and the inset flag; 17 Select checks), recorded through the lane's `--results`; the Simulator device was deleted. Visual regression: 88 shots changed or were added, all intended (Select 22 and 24 new, Dialog 16, Tooltip 8, the forced-colours rings of Checkbox, Fieldset, RadioGroup and Switch 18), then two compares with no differences (1,520 baselines); the sign-off set is 61 light renders.
- **Docs:** every Dialog and Tooltip example composes a Button into the trigger, and the pages say why (WCAG 2.2 2.5.8); whether a bare trigger gets a minimum size of its own is not decided. New sections for the default Close and focus return, Select in a Field, Field's button controls and the Vite dev server; the Deviations page's Select entries describe the shipped sheet, and it lists the nine new builder defaults.

Fixed: a form reset left Select's hidden `<select>` empty while the trigger showed the default (React Aria 1.22.1; the engine puts the value back after the browser's reset); the `<body>` warning could not fire (`<body>` was taken as the opener; it is no longer a step), and, found while fixing it, React Aria's hidden collection pass ran a second, popup-less chain (it now does nothing); the forced-colours ring on a selected Select row or checked Menu row was `CanvasText` on `Highlight` (about 1.9:1) and is `HighlightText`, one ring width inside the fill; a flush `Dialog.Close` example keeps the inset ring in the visual lane (`DialogPlainCloseExample`); the Dialog content's browser ring (above); the docs site painted light under a dark OS for an unknown stored theme (next-themes copied it onto `data-nz-theme`; it now writes only the class); the changeset `fixed` group named the not-yet-existing `@nizivo/charts` ([§5.4](#54-versioning-groups)); §7.4, D-al and `RELEASING.md` text; and the glyph table is tree-shakable (F1 in part: Dialog −173 B). **Week 7 status (2026-10-17).** The maintainer's decisions of 2026-10-16 (M18–M27, [§23.3](#233-maintainer-decisions-2026-10-14)), built by three builders (coarse-pointer text; Tabs direction; bare-trigger size) and a D-aa investigation, integrated into one tree. GitHub Actions stays off; nothing is committed, pushed, tagged or published by the integration. Measured numbers: `packages/react/W7-RESULTS.md`.
- **M25, 16 px text fields under a coarse pointer:** Input and Textarea switch to Body/L, NativeSelect and the Select trigger to Label/L under `@media (pointer: coarse)`, through a private font variable each (`--_nz-input-font` …); the public `--nz-*-font` hooks still win on every pointer. Heights stay 32/40/48 px; a Textarea row is 25.6 px. No new token: Body/L and Label/L exist. The floor lane gained 9 checks per scheme and `run.ts --touch` (Chromium and WebKit, results in `<engine>-<version>-touch.json`).
- **M26, Tabs direction:** `Tabs.Root` takes `dir?: 'ltr' | 'rtl'` (the old passthrough `string` is narrowed, so `dir="auto"` no longer typechecks); the arrows follow it, else the nearest enclosing `Tabs.Root`'s `dir` (a nested strip), else the scope locale. The carrier is a locale given to the tab list only (each tab's content gets the scope locale back), because WebKit 27.2 reads `Intl.Locale` direction from the language alone ([§16.4](#164-internationalisation-and-rtl-gap-report-b-part-a)); `NizivoConfig` keeps no direction key.
- **M27, bare triggers:** a `Dialog.Trigger` or `Tooltip.Trigger` with no `render` adds `nz-hit-target`, and its CSS gives it a 24 × 24 px minimum (`1.5rem`); measured 23.42 px tall before in Chromium, at least 24 px after in Chromium, WebKit and Firefox ([§15.1](#151-baseline)).
- **D-aa:** investigated, nothing changed: no design without a new import path met the maintainer's condition ([§23.4](#234-builder-defaults-2026-10-14) D-aa has the numbers and the trade-off).
- **Lanes:** the floor lane on Firefox 129.0 (re-downloaded free from Mozilla's release archive, same SHA-256), Chromium 156, WebKit 27.2 and Mobile Safari 17.5 passes 285 of 285 per scheme on the three week 5 pages and 20 of 20 on `w3-controls`, recorded through `--results`; Mobile Safari 17.5 and the `--touch` runs read 16 px in every field. The Simulator devices were deleted. Visual regression: 1,528 shots, no differences in two compares (no baseline changed; every baselined trigger composes a Button, and the shots are fine-pointer). Next (four modes), Vite and Astro pass on a fresh pack and were recorded with `--record` (0 failures, console messages, build warnings and axe violations in any mode; 68 of 68 `light-dark()` values; no theme flash). Overlay conformance Chromium 127 passed and 7 skipped, WebKit and Firefox 121 and 13; the browser lane Chromium 115 of 115 and Firefox 97 and 18 skipped after the review (WebKit's 7 Tab failures on macOS predate the week).
- **Size:** within every §17.1 limit; Tooltip 16,372 B is 12 B under its 16 KiB, Tabs 33,683 B, Dialog 32,127 B, the dashboard set 77,373 B (451 B under 76 KiB). `measure.ts --write` re-recorded 16 entries' `measured` (no limit moves). One budget was raised, in a labelled size commit of its own: `own: _engines/react-aria/tabs.client`, 629 → 769 B, over its 0.7 KiB, now 0.9 KiB (measured + 10%; not a §17.1 entry). The integration had run `--rebase`, which also raised five budgets that still held (`own: dialog/dialog.client` 1309 B of 1.4 KiB, `css/dialog.css` 944 B of 1 KiB, `css/input.css` 844 B of 0.9 KiB, `css/tooltip.css` 581 B of 0.6 KiB, `styles.css` 14,971 B of 16 KiB); the week 7 review put those back (F5).

**Week 30 status (2026-10-27, in progress).** v0.3 week 8: the Base UI canary (M65, D-dk). `src/_engines/base-ui/` holds a second overlay family for Dialog and Select on Base UI 1.9.0 (a devDependency of `@nizivo/react`, never built or shipped), swapped in together by `NZ_ADAPTER=base-ui` and run through the same overlay conformance specs by the non-blocking lane `overlay-base-ui-chromium` (27 lanes). First run: 48 of 78 cases pass, 25 fail, 5 skip (the mixed-family nesting cases). The Dialog passes its own spec; it drifts in O-04 (Dialog on Dialog). The Select's failures come from a few causes: Base UI keeps a Select's popup mounted (hidden) after its first open, where the contract says a closed popup is gone; its form control is a hidden `<input>`, not a native `<select>`; its trigger is `role="combobox"`; and it shows the raw value until the items have mounted. Results: `packages/react/W30-RESULTS.md`.

Profile A and the hash hold on every page in all three; Profile B holds on the server-safe pages; the one server-rendered `style=` attribute is React Aria's visually hidden wrapper of the Select's hidden `<select>` (R13); Trusted Types reports nothing. A unit test pins the theme script's published hash. The RTL mirror-geometry spec (`test/overlay/rtl.spec.tsx`) checks that `side="start"`/`"end"` lands on the mirrored physical edge for Popover, Menu and Tooltip, Select's `align="start"` follows the start edge, and Pagination's arrows flip, in all three engines. **Week 32 status (2026-10-27).** The canary after M74: the Base UI Select adapter now reads its items' labels up front (Base UI's `items`), renders its Portal only while the popup is open or exiting (Base UI force-mounts it, hidden, once the trigger has focus, for typeahead on a closed trigger; that typeahead is the cost), labels the listbox by its trigger (Base UI leaves it unnamed) and derives an option's focus-visible from the last input (`modality.client.tsx`). The select-field spec reads "a hidden form control" (a native `<select>` or an `<input>`) and accepts a combobox trigger, as M74 decided. The canary passes 68 of 78 (from 48); the 5 left are Base UI behaviour: O-01 (Select in Dialog), O-04 (Dialog on Dialog), a form reset that does not restore the Select's value, no page scroll lock while a Select is open, and its entry is not the spring. The default overlay lane passes 247 in Chromium and 238 in WebKit and Firefox. Results: `packages/react/W30-RESULTS.md` ("After M74").

**Week 34 status (2026-10-27): 0.3.0 release prep.** The release flow was rehearsed in a throwaway worktree: `pnpm changeset version` takes all four packages to 0.3.0 with every pending changeset in the changelogs; `--freeze` writes the 99 items to `apps/docs/registry/v0.3.0/` and refuses a second freeze; the docs build then serves `/r/v0.3.0/` and `/r/latest/` byte for byte as frozen, `_headers` names v0.3.0 and the items depend on `~0.3.0`. Nothing of the rehearsal was committed. `pnpm release` now checks the freeze in step 3: a real run blocks when its version is not frozen or when the frozen folder differs from what the commit builds (`packages/tooling/release/registry-freeze.ts`); a dry run notes a missing freeze. RELEASING.md gives the run time since v0.3 (25 to 30 minutes) and the freeze as the last change before the publish. Next, the maintainer: the dry run from `main`, then `pnpm release --publish` (version commit), `--freeze` and its commit, the publish and the tags, then `pnpm docs:deploy` and `--deploy`.

**0.3.0 released (2026-10-28).** The maintainer published `@nizivo/tokens`, `@nizivo/icons`, `@nizivo/react` and `@nizivo/charts` 0.3.0 with `pnpm release --publish`, pushed the four tags and moved `latest` to 0.3.0 (`next` and `latest` both 0.3.0). The registry was frozen at v0.3.0 first; its freeze commit failed `pnpm check` on formatting (fixed: `apps/docs/registry/**` is outside the formatter, and a test checks every frozen version against its manifest). The docs, the registry included, were deployed to Cloudflare Pages by the maintainer (M67) and are live at nizivo.neevtechnologies.in: every page answers, an unknown path 404s, `/r/v0.3.0/` is cached a year as immutable and `/r/latest/` five minutes, all 200 registry files match their manifest, and `shadcn add` of `init` and `product-card` from the live URLs installs `@nizivo/react` 0.3.0 from npm. Next: the lazy chunk before the DateRangePicker or the CommandMenu leaves `unstable/` (M71, M72), the canary's five Base UI differences, Dayitava's review of the v0.3 renders, and the hooks Tooltip default on 2026-11-06 (M68).

Client parts: FeedbackBar, ApprovalCard, ModelSelector (on Select), InlineAssist, PromptComposer. ResponseBlock's answer is one polite live region, busy while it streams, so it is read once; ChatLog is `role="log"`; ApprovalCard moves focus to Cancel when it appears, as Figma asks (`autoFocus`, default true); ModelSelector's trigger is named "Balanced Model", its visible text included. Figma's Glow Orb is a WebGPU shader, which stays with `@nizivo/lab` (§14.5): VoiceOrb and AiWelcome draw the orb with CSS gradients and take an `orb` slot. Decisions taken under M77, each reversible: the `./locales/en-US` limit 1.2 → 1.6 KiB (every AI part adds strings) and `css/base.css` 1.4 → 1.6 KiB (the new focus selectors); ResponseBlock's Stop and Retry always show; the docs show an example that passes an inline handler as source only, since the docs render examples as Server Components. The browser specs (`ai-kit*.spec.tsx`, 21 cases) pass in Chromium, Firefox and WebKit, 516 new visual baselines are stable on reruns, and no existing baseline moved. Not built: the assistant panel (7411:607). It is a pattern, so a registry item people copy, and it would copy code built on `unstable/` subpaths, which may change in a minor, with stylesheets that `init`'s `styles.css` does not carry; it waits until the AI parts are promoted (M77, reversible). Results: `packages/react/W36-RESULTS.md`.

**Week 37 status (2026-10-28, overnight under M77).** v0.5 (D-do): the unbuilt parts of Figma's 11 Data Display and 14 Product Patterns pages, under `@nizivo/react/unstable/<name>`. Server parts: Tag, Rating, NotificationItem, FileItem, Comment, EventCard, ProfileCard. Client parts: Slider, on a native `<input type="range">` with its fill set through the CSSOM (CSP-clean), and MediaPlayer, controls only, wired by the app to its media. Where they differ from Figma: the disabled Tag's name and the Comment's time keep text/secondary (AA); MediaPlayer's play button names its action without `aria-pressed`; ProfileCard's cover is CSS gradients in place of the Aurora shader (§14.5). The browser specs (`product-parts.spec.tsx`, `media.spec.tsx`, 11 cases) pass in Chromium, Firefox and WebKit, 236 new visual baselines are stable, and no existing baseline moved. Still to build from §21.4a: 13 Marketing, 16 Docs Site and Link. Results: `packages/react/W37-RESULTS.md`.

**Week 38 status (2026-10-28, overnight under M77).** The rest of §21.4a that the library owns, under `@nizivo/react/unstable/<name>`. v0.5 (D-do) adds Link and the Docs Site parts: DocsCard, CopyButton (announces "Copied") and CodeBlock (versions as the kit's Tabs). v0.6 (D-dp) is Figma's Marketing page: AnnouncementBar, SectionHeader, FeatureCard, StatBlock, TestimonialCard, CtaBand, SiteFooter, NewsletterSignup, BentoTile and SiteNavbar (its narrow menu a native `<details>`). Shader backgrounds are CSS stand-ins with a `background` slot until `@nizivo/lab` (§14.5). Under M77, `./locales/en-US` rose to 1.8 KiB; whether unstable parts' strings belong in the main catalogue is left to the maintainer. The browser specs (`docs-parts.spec.tsx`, `marketing.spec.tsx`, 10 cases) pass in Chromium, Firefox and WebKit, 356 new visual baselines are stable when recorded, and no existing baseline moved; in the full visual lane, 32 CodeBlock and CopyButton shots (dark and forced modes) differed when they ran after BentoTile. Fixed on 2026-10-28: any shot taller than the 720 px viewport went through Playwright's full-page capture, which left the page rendering CodeBlock's code text thinner for the rest of the run; the harness now grows the viewport for such a shot instead, and the full lane passes (`W38-RESULTS.md`). Then page 12's CSS motion parts (D-dq): Ticker, CaretHeadline, LivePulse and WordRotator, every loop on `--nz-duration-pulse` and stopped under Reduced motion. Not built: the Docs Header (the docs app's own), the AI assistant panel (week 36) and page 12's shaders (`@nizivo/lab`). Results: `packages/react/W38-RESULTS.md`.

- **Registry** (decision 5): `init`; commerce, storefront and template items; a `data-table` block (TanStack Table v9); `<name>-css` re-skin items; `<name>-source` fork items for components without an engine. Items depend on `@nizivo/*@~x.y.0`; item source is MIT. Immutable URLs, SHA-256 manifest, item-policy linter.
- **`unstable/*`:** DateRangePicker/RangeCalendar, CommandMenu + `@nizivo/react/hotkeys`.
- **New components:** Combobox, NumberField, QuantityStepper, Accordion (`<details name>`), DataView, BulkActionBar, PageIndicator, `Surface material="glass"`, `usePointerFx`, Funnel, Heatmap.
- **Base UI canary**: Dialog and Select adapters on one Base UI `OverlayEngine`, nightly.
- CSP Profile A/B fixtures; Trusted Types fixture (report-only); TanStack Start fixture (RSC lane non-blocking); RTL mirror-geometry test. (Argos is not funded; decision 6.)

### 21.4 v1.0 — "Freeze the contracts" (≈ 6–7 months from start)

- **API review:** every component is promoted or explicitly stays in `unstable/*`. Props, vocabulary, classes, hooks, layers and token names freeze.
- **Overlays:** all of O-01…O-25 on three browser engines × React 19.2/19.3.
- **Screen readers:** Guidepup NVDA and VoiceOver nightly; blocking after 30 green days.
- **Visual regression:** dark, mobile and RTL (ar-EG plus the `en-XA` pseudo-locale).
- **Accessibility debt:** every override is resolved in Figma or re-approved.
- **Supply chain:** SBOM + `actions/attest@v4`.
- **Governance:** `GOVERNANCE.md`; LTS policy published.
- **Design:** ghosts relinked; sign-off on breakpoints, dark elevation, the text ladder, toast timing, Card rest elevation, sidebar widths and the spring order; the cleaned Figma Community file.
- `@nizivo/codemod` only if a deprecation needs one.

### 21.4a After v0.3 — the pages Figma added on 2026-10-18 (M54)

- **AI** (Figma's 15 AI page, 7409:7): AI Badge, Suggestion Chip and Suggestion Group, Thinking Indicator, Response Block (Thinking, Streaming, Complete, Error), Feedback Bar, Citation, Source Card, Tool Call Card (Running, Done, Failed), Approval Card, Inline Assist, Model Selector, Model Menu, Voice Orb, Usage Meter and AI Welcome, and the assistant panel composition (7411:607). The accent colour stays AI-only (Figma's AI Badge rule).
- **With them,** the other pages added on 2026-10-18 ([figma/14](figma/14-changes-2026-10-18.md)): 11 Data Display, 12 Shaders & Motion, 13 Marketing, 14 Product Patterns, 16 Docs Site, about 60 components in all, none built. Not before v0.3 (M54: "no i dont want to pre pone AI components").

### 21.5 Explicitly deferred

| Deferred | Why it is safe | What brings it back |
|---|---|---|
| `@nizivo/lab` (shaders, liquid glass, 3D) | The 3D items are raster images; shader sources are unreadable; SVG liquid glass is Chromium-only | A consumer request; independent 0.x |
| Native top-layer overlay adapters | Four platform gates ([§12.4](#124-native-swap-gates)) | The gates clear; one component at a time |
| A Motion (motion.dev) adapter | CSS covers every Figma motion spec; Motion costs 44.2 KB and its SSR leaves content invisible | Consumers can already wrap parts through `render` |
| `unstyled` prop | CSS-file granularity covers it | A consumer request (additive) |
| Brand and density values | No consumer and no Figma source | A real second brand (resolver modifier) |
| A Safari 16.4 token floor (duplicated theme blocks) | Floor is 17.5; block selectors cannot express inverse surfaces inside nested themes; the `light-dark()` theme format is public ([§4.2](#42-seam-ledger)) | A consumer request; ships as a major |
| `@nizivo/css` | No non-React consumer | A second framework or plain-HTML consumer |
| React Compiler output | Crashes under `react-server` | Compile phase 2 for `*.client.tsx` only, after benchmarks |
| Code Connect | Needs an Organization plan | An Organization upgrade (the map converts mechanically) |
| Native Temporal | Not Baseline (Safari `preview`) | web-features marks it Baseline (a patch) |
| ~~A 12 px caption style~~ | No longer deferred: decision 8c (2026-10-14) allows 12 px through the `Body/S` and `Label/S` proposals ([§7.6](#76-code-owned-tokens)) | — |
| Hotspot editor, Vue or Web Components bindings | Out of scope | Product demand |

## 22. Risks and mitigations

| # | Risk | Likelihood / impact | Mitigation | Revisit when |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| R1 | **The design keeps churning** (it is a work in progress) | High / medium | `unstable/*`; dark as preview; provisional `lg`; patterns in docs and the registry, not npm; one-click token sync with a diff; 0.x semantics; promotion after two unchanged sync cycles | Every two weeks |
| R2 | **Team bandwidth**; week 1 is heavy | High / high | The ordered cut line; a minimal generator first; one engine; three packages; registry, lab and RTL visual regression deferred; 2-week buffer | Every two weeks |
| R3 | **Figma ownership** (the file stays on "Vampire D Studio", a Professional team Neev does not formally own; decision 3) | Medium / high | Accepted under zero spend. A Neev person keeps edit or admin access; every sync commits the raw export to the repo; a `.fig` backup at each minor; TokensBrücke works on any plan; MCP path optional; never poll a Starter file | Loss of access to the team, or a plan downgrade |
| R4 | **The engine contract drifts toward React Aria-only behaviour**, so a swap stops being cheap | Medium / high | Contract minimalism (two engines or native); `'unknown'` reasons; second Tooltip adapter in v0.2; Base UI Dialog + Select canary (one overlay core) in v0.3; conformance asserts classes, `data-slot` and exactly the vocabulary `data-*` (`engineDom`); engine-owned strings listed per component | v0.2, v0.3 |
| R5 | **The S2 export shape regresses** (someone namespaces from a client file; a tsdown change) | Medium / high (production build breaks) | Generated barrels only; the `export * as` lint; directive guard; Next fixture on both bundlers on every PR; exact tsdown pin | Each tsdown upgrade |
| R6 | **Untested on the real floor:** Safari 17.5 itself (only WebKit 27.2 ran), and Firefox for the CSP matrix and the D-spike. W1: the floor lane verified Firefox 129 for S1, the probe and overlay entry | Medium / high | Week-1 floor lane (done for Firefox); v0.1 exit criterion 6 and its free Safari routes (the iOS 17.5 Simulator is installed since 2026-10-15, and week 4 runs the lane on it); documented degradations (Deviations page entries D1 and D2, [§18.2](#182-docs-site-public)) | A Safari 17.5 run |
| R7 | **TokensBrücke output fails Terrazzo's validation**, or typography names differ | Medium / medium | Day-1 real export (W1: the raw export fails `tz check` on its own and passes once normalized; names recorded in `DRY-RUN.md`); the normalizer writes the final form and `tz format` is a check; golden files; `subValueVariableName` and the `contract` plugin rename; MCP snapshot fallback | First plugin sync |
| R8 | **Terrazzo** (small community; broke at 0.10 → 2.0) changes output | Medium / medium | Exact pin; golden files; custom logic in plugins; the DTCG source stays tool-neutral; the Style Dictionary swap is documented but UNVERIFIED | Each Terrazzo minor |
| R9 | **tsdown is pre-1.0; pnpm 12 OIDC through Changesets is untested** | Medium / high | Exact pins; shared preset; directive guard; exports drift; publint/attw; the OIDC path is off the 0.1.0 path (M14: 0.1.0 publishes by hand, so the local release script's first run is the 0.1.0 risk) and is rehearsed before the first OIDC release; pnpm 11.28.x fallback; unbundled output so tsc or bunchee can replace tsdown with no consumer impact | Each minor |
| R10 | **React Aria toast hooks change** (stability UNVERIFIED) or the new Sheet is buggy | Medium / medium | Own store and region; hooks only render; O-06/O-07/O-09/O-14 gate upgrades; one Renovate group; Sheet falls back to Modal + CSS | Each React Aria minor |
| R11 | **React Aria Tabs weight** (31.9 KB) on Tabs-only pages | Medium / low | `TabNav` for link tabs (0 KB); budgets; the hooks escape hatch | A budget failure |
| R12 | **The a11y override layer becomes permanent debt** | Medium / medium | Expiry, redundancy and drift checks; the public deviations page; the release gate | Each expiry date |
| R13 | **CSP:** React Aria's inline `VisuallyHidden` styles break under Profile B | High for Profile B users / medium | Profile A is the default; B is guaranteed for server-safe parts; per-release grep of engine inline styles | v0.3 fixtures |
| R14 | **Percent-space SVG in Safari** (`non-scaling-stroke`), clip-path draw in RTL | Low / medium (was medium; the stroke check passed in WebKit 27.2 on 2026-10-19, also under `scale: -1 1` and after a clip-path reveal; **since week 10 it is a browser spec**, `packages/charts/test/browser/stroke.spec.tsx`, green in Chromium, Firefox and WebKit at DPR 1 and 2, and in the `charts-*` release lanes) | Pixel-space fallback kept in reserve; `xDirection` default `ltr`; real Safari and the 17.5 floor still to check | v0.2 week 1 |
| R15 | **Locale-string weight** (+4.8 KB on the dashboard set; +13.4 KB for date pickers) without the optimise plugin | High / low | Budgets measured with all locales; per-framework plugin recipes | — |
| R16 | **The Safari 17.5 floor sits above Next 16 and Tailwind 4 (16.4)** | Low / medium | Documented; a lower floor needs a new theme mechanism and ships as a major ([§4.2](#42-seam-ledger), [§21.5](#215-explicitly-deferred)) | A consumer asks |
| R17 | **Spring parameter order is wrong** | Low / low | Golden test pins the interpretation; the maintainer accepted the order on 2026-10-14 (M12); a fix is a token patch | Design reports a different order |
| R18 | **Toast outlet into native modals** (consumer `<dialog>`) misbehaves (state loss, duplicate announcements) | Medium / medium | Only needed for consumer native modals today; gated by O-08 at v1.0; documented limitation until then | v1.0 |
| R19 | **Visual-regression cost or terms** | Low / low | In-repo screenshots only (decision 6, zero spend); baselines in plain Git until they outgrow it | Baselines too large for plain Git |
| R20 | **Licence chosen after outside contributions** | Low / high | v0.1 gate: decided before the repo opens | Week 0 |
